# Agents · S · part 18

938 entries, ship-devops-reviewer to sima-yi. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ship-devops-reviewer**: Expert DevOps/release reviewer for the ship pipeline's QA and prod-prep phases. Audits deploy repeatability, environment hygiene, backups + restore, monitoring/alerting, domain/TLS/redirects, and rollback. Use during phase-4 QA or phase-6 prod-prep to confirm the project can be launched and operated safely. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-devops-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ship-e2e**: E2E Agent — generate and execute browser-based tests against Feature Brief user journeys ([dain-agency/mood-tracker](https://github.com/dain-agency/mood-tracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ship-e2e.md))
- **ship-evaluator**: Active acceptance-testing agent for /ship runs. Use when the goal has user-visible behavior — runs the app, exercises it like a real user (browser/preview tools or CLI), and grades every contract criterion pass/fail with evidence. Never edits code. ([adhikari-sandeep/ship-harness](https://github.com/adhikari-sandeep/ship-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/evaluator.md) · ★1)
- **ship-fixer**: Applies the review findings the user picked, or kills surviving mutants, for /ship-ticket. Launched only by the ship-ticket orchestrator. ([agrorithms/destiny-public](https://github.com/agrorithms/destiny-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ship-fixer.md))

- **ship-gate**: GATE worker for /sy:ship: a lightweight convergence loop that delegates the frontier verdict to `sy:gate`, CI triage to `/sy:ci`, and review-thread reconciliation to `/sy:pr`, drives the fix cycle, and promotes. ([nearmap/shipyard](https://github.com/nearmap/shipyard/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-gate.md) · ★1)
- **ship-gate-judge**: LLM-as-judge for ship pipeline gates. Scores a phase's artifact against a fixed rubric (0-100) and returns pass/fail vs a threshold, with concrete reasons. Use at a gate for artifact-heavy phases (research, spec, QA) to turn "looks done" into an objective, recorded score before the owner confirms. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-gate-judge.md) · ★1)
- **ship-gates**: What each gate in the ship profile's `## Local gate` runs and why. The profile ([ship.md](ship.md)) keeps the lines a run reads at its start; this file is the reference for a run that has a gate to read about. Vocabulary: [GLOSSARY.md](../../GLOSSARY.md). ([Gharib89/skills](https://github.com/Gharib89/skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ship-gates.md))
- **ship-guard**: Pre-commit safety reviewer for the two-repo DevOpsWorker workspace. Reads the staged diff and answers what the commit hook can't cheaply judge: (1) are these files in the RIGHT repo — generic pipeline code to the public core, site-specific agents/deploy/registries/docs to the private overlay? (2) does the diff leak customer/tenant names, internal repo URLs or paths, internal tool names, environment IDs, or secrets? Use before committing, especially to the public core. Read-only — reports, never edits or commits. ([SShadowS/DevOpsWorker](https://github.com/SShadowS/DevOpsWorker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ship-guard.md) · ★11)
- **ship-implementer**: Build agent for /ship runs. - **ship-mate-playwright**: Playwright E2E tester for the ShipMate pipeline. - **ship-scan**: You assemble evidence; you decide nothing. - **ship-security**: Revue de sécurité d'une branche, limitée au diff et à ce qu'il touche — permissions, isolation des données, validation d'entrée, secrets, appels sortants, données personnelles (RGPD). Rend PASS ou FIX avec des findings prouvés. Lecture seule. À lancer dans /ship en parallèle de la revue produit et de la code-review, avec la branche, .ship/&lt;slug&gt;/ et le profil de repo. ([MielPopsssssss/claude-setup](https://github.com/MielPopsssssss/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/bundle/agents/ship-security.md))
- **ship-security-reviewer**: Read-only security review agent for /ship runs. Use on a diff after implementation — OWASP-style pass over changed code, secrets, injection, authz, data exposure. Never edits; returns structured findings. ([adhikari-sandeep/ship-harness](https://github.com/adhikari-sandeep/ship-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/security-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ship-security-reviewer**: Expert application-security reviewer for the ship pipeline's QA phase. Audits authn/authz, injection, SSRF, secret handling, the untrusted-data boundary, and webhook/billing integrity. Use during phase-4 expert QA before any launch, especially for public SaaS and anything handling money or PII. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-security-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ship-seo-expert**: Expert SEO/AEO reviewer for the ship pipeline's QA phase. Audits a site or app for indexability, on-page metadata, structured data, canonicalization, content quality, and answer-engine optimization. Use during phase-4 expert QA of any content site or SaaS with a marketing surface. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-seo-expert.md) · ★1)
- **ship-seo-strategist**: Go-to-market / SEO strategist for the ship research phase (and revisited in monitor). Researches keyword demand, competitor SERP landscape, audience channels, and a distribution + content/programmatic-SEO plan for how to actually get the project found and adopted. Distinct from ship-seo-expert, which audits on-page SEO of built pages — this one plans promotion. Use for anything with a marketing surface. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-seo-strategist.md) · ★1)

Success is the configured enqueue outcome; final merge and issue closure remain asynchronous repository events. - **short-story-forge-planner**: 短篇小说结构设计师。根据题材/灵感设计完整故事结构、场景序列、情绪曲线。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to plan a short story. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-planner.md))
- **short-story-forge-publisher**: 短篇小说发布运营编辑。QA通过后生成微头条和推广物料，用于今日头条推广。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to generate publishing materials. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-publisher.md))
- **short-story-forge-qa**: 短篇小说质量审查员。对最终正文执行硬性质量检查：禁词扫描、字数统计、标点检查、完整性验证。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator for final quality check. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-qa.md))
- **short-story-forge-styler**: 短篇小说文风打磨师。深度去AI化，优化节奏感、句式变化、口语化程度，让文字读起来像真人作者。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to polish writing style. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-styler.md))
- **short-story-forge-writer**: 短篇小说正文写手。根据Planner的结构计划写8000-12000字中文短篇正文。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to write story content. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-writer.md))

- **shot-judge**: 시안의 화면·설명·흐름을 실물로 검수한다. editorial-concept는 전체 시청 경험과 개념 표현을, script-faithful는 제공 대본에 대한 비트별 A/B 적합성을 판정한다. ([azuri-pixel/shortform-news-liveaction](https://github.com/azuri-pixel/shortform-news-liveaction/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/shot-judge.md))
- **shot-reviewer**: Judges rendered frames or contact sheets of a VJ scene against a written rubric and returns one JSON verdict per shot. - **shuffle-determinism-auditor**: Read-only auditor that finds Go tests whose result depends on the shuffle. Use after writing tests for a card game, and before pushing a change that adds a branch to a message string or a hand evaluation. MUST BE USED when a change adds a new game or a new deal-dependent branch. ([yuta-yoshinaga/go_trumpcards](https://github.com/yuta-yoshinaga/go_trumpcards/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shuffle-determinism-auditor.md) · ★2)

Official dsh-cc-shunt plugin build; spawns on the haiku alias when the deployment configures it. - **sim-reviewer**: Monte Carlo simulation reviewer. Checks the parts of a simulation study that general R review misses — the assumption regime a run is in, DGP/estimand alignment, replication budget and Monte Carlo standard error, coverage computed against the truth, parallel-seed discipline, and whether headline simulation claims match both the generated tables and the regime that can support them. Use after writing or modifying a Monte Carlo simulation script, or as the review pass inside /simulation-study. ([pedrohcgs/claude-code-my-workflow](https://github.com/pedrohcgs/claude-code-my-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sim-reviewer.md) · ★1.7k · 3 copies)

- **sim-runner**: Äger spårets körkatalog och stack. Uppdaterar körkatalogen, startar och stoppar spårets separata stack, bygger om, applicerar migrationer där, kör simuleringsscenarier och nativesviter, och skriver utkast till status.json per scenario-ID. Den enda agenttypen som får köra mot eller skriva till stacken. ([heke99/gridex-ops-platform](https://github.com/heke99/gridex-ops-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sim-runner.md))

- **sima-yi**: Invoke Sima Yi for questions about strategic patience, competitive endurance, timing of decisive action, long-game positioning, and building durable organisations under sustained pressure. Best when the mentee faces an aggressive rival, a premature-action temptation, a succession or delegation question, or needs to think through the right moment to move vs. hold. Primary domains: Strategy & Competition, Business & Leadership. ([jakapolr/world-philosophers](https://github.com/jakapolr/world-philosophers/blob/HEAD/agents/sima-yi.md) · ★1)
